# Existing landfill cell field-welded LLDPE composite final cover construction and closure certification services

## 1. Scope and Applicability

This PCR covers completed construction and closure certification of a field-welded LLDPE composite final cover on an identified retained waste-filled landfill cell. An approved low-permeability soil or GCL component in composite contact with LLDPE is required by this scope; actual designed drainage, gas, protection and vegetative layers are separately identified. Start after acceptance of the existing filled-cell baseline, not a new landfill construction project. Actual permit, closure design and CQA requirements govern the work and responsible certification. Sources describe physical roles; historical Rose Hill RCRA/CERCLA case requirements are not universal MSWLF rules.

State survey convention and include actual slopes once. Normalize attributed inventory to 1 m2. Do not sum layers, overlaps, repairs, roll areas or prepare handoff areas. |
| native_units | all inventory rows | Actual compatible verified property | actual native unit | Preserve each actual supplier and return SKU in native Area, Length, Count, Volume, Mass, Energy or provider service unit. Confirm matching support identities independently. Native_state governs nonadditive density/moisture conversions with actual state and temperature; energy, time and price cannot be forced into counted service or mass. |

| scope_gate | all processes | Own actual grading, subgrade conditioning/compaction, approved soil or GCL plus LLDPE composite barrier, separately designed gas/drainage/protection/erosion layers and all connections, field seaming, tests, repair and certification. The gate requires plan-conforming final layer survey, material traceability, actual required non-destructive/destructive seam tests, penetration/anchorage and drainage/gas inspections, erosion/vegetation establishment where required, defect closure, as-builts and signed responsible engineer/owner/authority acceptance. Criteria, layer thicknesses, soil moisture/compaction limits, test frequencies, climatic restrictions and certification roles come from the actual applicable approved plan; this PCR prescribes no construction engineering settings. | `epa-mswlf-closure-requirements`; `frtr-landfill-soil-capping`; `rid-em-rosehill-cqa-2006` |
| baseline_increment | all processes | Declare retained waste, intermediate cover, existing gas and leachate equipment, background decomposition and baseline gas/water flows before work. No historical waste manufacture is purchased again. Own actual incremental grading/removal, disturbance releases, temporary capture/pumping/treatment, damage, contamination control and reinstatement during the job. Establish attribution by comparable baseline and job/event measurements with confounders and uncertainty; missing attribution is a material data gap, never an automatic zero or an assumed net reduction. | `epa-mswlf-closure-requirements`; `frtr-landfill-soil-capping`; `rid-em-rosehill-cqa-2006` |
| owned_resources | all processes | Include actual direct and subcontracted resources, rental/provider assets, mobilization/demobilization, freight including required empty legs, temporary utilities, sampling, destructive coupons, retries, clean-up and stock exits once. Decompose upstream service coverage before adding physical purchases. A factory composite component includes its factory constituents. Landfill operating waste placement, new cell/bottom liner or whole landfill, daily/intermediate cover, soil-only evapotranspiration covers and later postclosure monitoring/maintenance are excluded. | `epa-mswlf-closure-requirements`; `frtr-landfill-soil-capping`; `rid-em-rosehill-cqa-2006` |
| internal_handoffs | all processes | The prepare-to-cap subgrade handoff is an internal state transition linked by transfer_id, not another external purchase or final reference output. Record actual prepared and accepted footprints separately. A same-category bought closure-cover service is bounded by its actual certified gate and complete upstream scope; do not recursively buy this project again. | `epa-mswlf-closure-requirements`; `frtr-landfill-soil-capping`; `rid-em-rosehill-cqa-2006` |

## 6. Process Inventory Structure

### Process Map

| process_id | process_name | inclusion | inclusion_condition | role | quantitative_reference |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| prepare | Existing cell subgrade preparation and qualification | required | Every covered project | Actual incoming soil/baseline conditioning to independently inspected qualified subgrade; recondition rejected zones before cap release | Measured prepared developed area, soil states and acceptance record; internal handoff |
| cap | Composite cap installation CQA and closure certification | required | After qualified prepare handoff | Integrate approved barrier and other designed layers, field weld/test/repair and certify final cover | Certified unique net developed final cover area A |

### Process: Subgrade preparation and qualification (`prepare`)

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Approved separately supplied grading and subgrade soil product (`prep_soil_grading_soil`)

This card is only the independently supplied approved separately supplied grading and subgrade soil product in its actual SKU, material/formulation, physical state and supply gate. Activate only if it actually occurs and is outside complete assembly/provider coverage. Its composition, dimensions or count are qualifiers or nonadditive auxiliary measurements, never another purchase. The other objects formerly grouped with it are mandatory separately resolved requirements under atom_req_prepare_prep_soil. No parent family total or numerical Range is assigned to this child. Range evidence need: collect this object's independent kg receipts, attributed opening/closing stock, usable returns, final installed/use, waste/loss and measurement uncertainty at the actual event and custody point. The same actual SKU/state/supply gate used for several functions has only one acquisition owner; merge its physical supply record once and treat the other use role as nonadditive, rather than repeating that receipt in multiple child cards.

- Selected flow: Approved separately supplied grading and subgrade soil product
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: Use this child SKU/state independently measured stock-adjusted supply in kg, not the parent total: attributed opening + external receipts after tare - carried closing. Retain nonreturned waste/loss upstream; use exactly the original declared gross-with-outlets or consumed-only matched-return convention. Normalize once by the actual accepted reference denominator defined in sections 3–4 and its gate protocol. Internal handoff children use their independently measured same-SKU dispatch/receipt instead of another external acquisition.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_atom_prep_soil_grading_soil`

###### Complete-life equivalent share of actual excavator (`prepare_assets_excavator`)

This is the lifecycle-equivalent share of one actually identified complete-life equivalent share of actual excavator, with manufacture, maintenance, replacements and end-of-life across all consuming jobs/nodes/periods. It is never one full machine purchased for this job. Use one asset ID, actual compatible job/node driver and evidenced all-consumer complete-life driver in the same unit. Observed completed life or observed history plus evidenced remaining use/replacements/termination/end-of-life needs source, uncertainty, sensitivity and update triggers; missing coverage keeps final foreground attribution incomplete. Exclude fuel, spares, provider coverage and factory-included components already owned elsewhere. A battery/charger included in a complete cutter burden is not bought again; separate asset shares require a reconciled nonoverlapping asset boundary. Range evidence need: actual asset-specific lifecycle burden and compatible numerator/denominator evidence; no assumed lifetime or default fraction.

- Selected flow: Complete-life equivalent share of actual excavator
- Flow property / unit: Number of items / Item(s)
- Amount rule: asset_equivalent_Item(s) = actual job_node_driver / evidenced all_consumer_full_life_driver in the same driver unit; quantity per reference = asset_equivalent_Item(s) / actual accepted reference denominator. Use an upstream full lifecycle burden per one complete asset Item(s); do not use a material stock formula, count a whole machine per job, substitute rental hours for Item(s), or normalize twice.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_atom_prepare_assets_excavator`

###### Qualified prepared retained-cell subgrade handoff (`prepared_subgrade`)

Internal handoff to cap assembly after actual grading, moisture conditioning and compaction/inspection required by design. Record actual accepted subgrade developed area and condition; it is not a separately sold final cover or an additional purchase. Rejected zones remain in prepare until corrected.

- Selected flow: Qualified prepared retained-cell subgrade handoff
- Flow property / unit: Area / m2
- Amount rule: Actual qualified prepared developed subgrade area divided once by A; nonadditive internal handoff, not fixed to 1 m2.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_prepare_gate`
- Sources: `epa-mswlf-closure-requirements`; `frtr-landfill-soil-capping`; `rid-em-rosehill-cqa-2006`

Range evidence need: Collect qualified prepared footprint, rejected-zone area, incoming and compacted soil states and independent qualification-inspection uncertainty; it need not equal final A.

###### Actually segregated discarded construction soil (`prepare_solid_waste_soil`)

This card is only the actual captured actually segregated discarded construction soil at its recorded dispatch state and receiver. Activate only when that stream is physically separately measured/handled; if materials remain in one mixed carrier, keep that actual mixture under the mandatory refinement ledger and do not also charge its included constituents here. Record wet/dry basis, contamination, state and recipient. Historical removed host material is not a new upstream purchase or negative material supply. No recycling/avoided-production credit, parent total or family Range is inferred. Range evidence need: independent net kg dispatch weight/measurement and receiver evidence, including actual moisture/composition and uncertainty.

- Selected flow: Actually segregated discarded construction soil
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: Actual separately measured dispatched waste in kg for this carrier/state/recipient divided once by the actual accepted reference denominator. Never use supply stock or the parent mixed-waste total as every child amount; retained repair/return/closing and contained constituents are excluded from duplicate disposal.

##### Elementary flows

###### Actual construction H2O vapour released to air unspecified (`prepare_water_release_h2o_vapour_air`)

Conditional actual H2O vapour emitted to air, unspecified, owned by this node. Activate only when evaporation occurrence, actual H2O species, state and this receiving-air compartment are evidenced. Independent measured water-only kg or a validated operation-specific mass model is required; an incomplete water balance or all supplied water is never this release. Captured wastewater, soil moisture, bound/free retained water, stock, liquid discharge and other-node releases remain exclusive fates. Range evidence need: own event/time/activity and actual release evidence with uncertainty; any auxiliary liquid-water volume-to-water-mass bridge requires actual compatible water content/density/state/temperature/source, not assumed gas density or wet-carrier mass.

- Selected flow: Actual construction H2O vapour released to air unspecified
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: Use this independently measured or validated actual H2O-to-air mass kg divided once by the original actual accepted reference denominator. Legacy water-volume ledger is auxiliary and nonadditive; reconcile only through actual same-event compatible water-state evidence. Unknown evaporation, density, water fraction, medium or balance term remains a gap, never zero or residual-inferred vapour.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_atom_prepare_water_release_h2o_vapour_air`

### Process: Composite cover installation and certification (`cap`)

#### Inputs

##### Product flows

###### Metered electricity supplied for cap (`cap_electricity`)

Record grid supply, temporary power and provider-metered demand with upstream scope. Fuel for a generator and its delivered electricity cannot both carry the same generation burden.

- Selected flow: Metered electricity supplied for cap
- Flow property / unit: Energy / kWh
- Amount rule: Actual attributable native quantity divided once by A, recorded per reference flow from the linked node protocol, with compatible stock and physical-state reconciliation.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_cap_energy`
- Sources:

Range evidence need: Collect phase-specific meter uncertainty, imports/exports, standby and provider-power scope; no default welding, pumping or compaction kWh-per-m2.

###### Actually completed bounded qualified earthwork service (`cap_services_earthwork`)

This is one declared, actually completed and accepted bounded actually completed bounded qualified earthwork service function, only where the provider defines an independently countable physical completed-result package in Item(s). Record its actual definition, result/gate, provider and full included material/energy/transport/asset/waste scope; owned uncovered resources remain explicit. Invoice line, labour time or fee is not this event count. A provider using another native reference remains an unresolved refinement requirement rather than being forced into Count. Range evidence need: actual accepted complete event count and scope evidence, no default one package per job.

- Selected flow: Actually completed bounded qualified earthwork service
- Flow property / unit: Number of items / Item(s)
- Amount rule: Independently count actual uniquely identified, completed and accepted service-result events under this one declared function and package definition; divide that actual event count once by the original actual accepted reference denominator. Do not use a material stock balance, full-asset count, labour hours, price or an assumed event count; reconcile any provider/direct overlap once.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_atom_cap_services_earthwork`

###### Complete-life equivalent share of actual excavator (`cap_assets_excavator`)

This is the lifecycle-equivalent share of one actually identified complete-life equivalent share of actual excavator, with manufacture, maintenance, replacements and end-of-life across all consuming jobs/nodes/periods. It is never one full machine purchased for this job. Use one asset ID, actual compatible job/node driver and evidenced all-consumer complete-life driver in the same unit. Observed completed life or observed history plus evidenced remaining use/replacements/termination/end-of-life needs source, uncertainty, sensitivity and update triggers; missing coverage keeps final foreground attribution incomplete. Exclude fuel, spares, provider coverage and factory-included components already owned elsewhere. A battery/charger included in a complete cutter burden is not bought again; separate asset shares require a reconciled nonoverlapping asset boundary. Range evidence need: actual asset-specific lifecycle burden and compatible numerator/denominator evidence; no assumed lifetime or default fraction.

- Selected flow: Complete-life equivalent share of actual excavator
- Flow property / unit: Number of items / Item(s)
- Amount rule: asset_equivalent_Item(s) = actual job_node_driver / evidenced all_consumer_full_life_driver in the same driver unit; quantity per reference = asset_equivalent_Item(s) / actual accepted reference denominator. Use an upstream full lifecycle burden per one complete asset Item(s); do not use a material stock formula, count a whole machine per job, substitute rental hours for Item(s), or normalize twice.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_atom_cap_assets_excavator`

###### Specified geosynthetic clay liner barrier component (`gcl_barrier`)

Include actual approved GCL when used as the low-permeability component in intimate composite contact with the LLDPE. Keep bentonite/carrier already in purchased GCL within that supplied component. Where approved soil replaces GCL, document absence and use atom_req_cap_cap_soil; drainage composite cannot silently replace this barrier duty.

- Amount rule: Actual attributable native quantity divided once by A, recorded per reference flow from the linked node protocol, with compatible stock and physical-state reconciliation.

##### Waste flows

###### Actually segregated discarded construction soil (`cap_solid_waste_soil`)

This card is only the actual captured actually segregated discarded construction soil at its recorded dispatch state and receiver. Activate only when that stream is physically separately measured/handled; if materials remain in one mixed carrier, keep that actual mixture under the mandatory refinement ledger and do not also charge its included constituents here. Record wet/dry basis, contamination, state and recipient. Historical removed host material is not a new upstream purchase or negative material supply. No recycling/avoided-production credit, parent total or family Range is inferred. Range evidence need: independent net kg dispatch weight/measurement and receiver evidence, including actual moisture/composition and uncertainty.

- Selected flow: Actually segregated discarded construction soil
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: Actual separately measured dispatched waste in kg for this carrier/state/recipient divided once by the actual accepted reference denominator. Never use supply stock or the parent mixed-waste total as every child amount; retained repair/return/closing and contained constituents are excluded from duplicate disposal.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_atom_cap_solid_waste_soil`

##### Elementary flows

###### Actual construction H2O vapour released to air unspecified (`cap_water_release_h2o_vapour_air`)

Conditional actual H2O vapour emitted to air, unspecified, owned by this node. Activate only when evaporation occurrence, actual H2O species, state and this receiving-air compartment are evidenced. Independent measured water-only kg or a validated operation-specific mass model is required; an incomplete water balance or all supplied water is never this release. Captured wastewater, soil moisture, bound/free retained water, stock, liquid discharge and other-node releases remain exclusive fates. Range evidence need: own event/time/activity and actual release evidence with uncertainty; any auxiliary liquid-water volume-to-water-mass bridge requires actual compatible water content/density/state/temperature/source, not assumed gas density or wet-carrier mass.

- Selected flow: Actual construction H2O vapour released to air unspecified
- Flow property / unit: Mass / kg
- Amount rule: Use this independently measured or validated actual H2O-to-air mass kg divided once by the original actual accepted reference denominator. Legacy water-volume ledger is auxiliary and nonadditive; reconcile only through actual same-event compatible water-state evidence. Unknown evaporation, density, water fraction, medium or balance term remains a gap, never zero or residual-inferred vapour.
- Value mode: `calculated_value`
- Specificity: `site_specific`
- Normalization basis: per reference flow
- Basis kind: `reference_flow`
- Evidence kind: `calculated_from_collection`
- Collection protocol: `cp_atom_cap_water_release_h2o_vapour_air`

## 7. Allocation and Co-product Handling

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| stock_account | all processes | For each native SKU/state, S = attributed_opening + external_net_tare_gross_receipts - carried_closing. Internal issue and installed activity are not receipts. Choose declared gross-with-outlets: input S and matched usable outward return separately, or consumed-only: input S minus matched usable outward return and no additional return burden subtraction or credit. In both views retain each independently measured physical outward-return record/card as an audit view; that physical record is not another modeled upstream subtraction or credit. The chosen supply and return burden views are nonadditive. Closing stock, recovered spill still held at close, contained waste, uncontained loss and usable external return are distinct disjoint fates. Nonreturned offcuts, rejects and losses retain their upstream burdens. Historic removed materials never reduce new purchases; no recycling or avoided-production credit is implied. |  |
| asset_share | all processes | For each shared plant/tool/test instrument and temporary installation, include manufacture, maintenance, replacements and end-of-life across ALL consumers, jobs, nodes and periods. Enumerate prepare/cap consumers and other jobs, calendar periods, setup/changeover/cleaning, replacements and termination. Choose one representation: native asset-equivalent quantity per reference flow = (job_driver / full_life_driver) / A with the full asset lifecycle burden in the matching native unit; OR lifecycle-complete per-driver service quantity per reference flow = job_driver / A with burden in the same driver unit. Do not combine them or divide twice. Full_life_driver requires observed life or observed history plus evidenced remaining use, future replacements, termination and end-of-life, source, uncertainty, sensitivity and update conditions. Job hours alone do not establish full-life service or a counted machine equivalent; missing life/coverage evidence blocks a completed dataset. |  |
| campaign_rework | all processes | Production is a project campaign indexed by cell, approved layer/zone, panel/weld or soil batch, event and period. Preparatory, placement, test/repair and final acceptance phases retain actual start/end dates. Attribute setup, changeover, idle/standby, cleaning and shared provider demand using evidenced causal physical drivers; retain failed/repeated work in this job numerator. Link failed soil tests to prepare reconditioning and failed seams/coupons/penetrations to cap repair/retest; identify retained repaired material, usable external return and actual waste exit at its producing node. Count final surface once only after defect closure. No default reject rate, annualization, postclosure life or credit for future avoided leachate/gas generation. | `epa-mswlf-closure-requirements`; `frtr-landfill-soil-capping`; `rid-em-rosehill-cqa-2006` |
| atom_grouped_ledgers | all actual-child records | Apply each existing formula only to its applicable role: material stock, asset lifecycle fraction/hourly provision, completed service event, internal transfer and species/fate calculations are distinct. Never apply material-stock or full-machine purchase equations to an asset share or service event. Within the applicable method group, use concrete child identity/material, state, one exchange property/unit, receiving medium and ownership/event scope. Native and converted views are nonadditive. A family diagnostic total is never a flow identity or every child numerator; keep incomplete portions explicit. Complete formulations, assemblies and genuinely bounded single-function services remain whole, with included constituents/resources not purchased again. |  |

## 8. Foreground Data Collection, Calculation, and Quality Rules

### Data Collection Protocols

| protocol_id | process_id | flow_role | record_type | raw_fields | collection_method | unit | frequency | temporal_coverage | site_scope | aggregation_rule | quality_evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| cp_prepare_stock | prepare | stock records owned by prepare | foreground_record | site_id; job_id; cell_id; process_id; campaign_id; batch_id; event_id; phase_id; period_id; date_start; date_end; owner; source_record; A; reference_unit; sku; lot_id; state; material_role; layer_id; transfer_id; attributed_opening; external_gross_receipts; tare; external_net_tare_gross_receipts; carried_closing; internal_issue; installed_activity; usable_outward_return; return_receipt_link; return_condition; accounting_view; quantity; native_unit; wet_volume; wet_volume_unit; wet_density; wet_density_unit; wet_density_source; wet_mass; wet_mass_unit; dry_mass; dry_mass_unit; loose_volume; loose_volume_unit; compacted_volume; compacted_volume_unit; density; density_unit; density_state; density_method; density_source; wet_basis_water_fraction; dry_basis_water_ratio; moisture_method; moisture_source; loose_dry_density; loose_dry_density_unit; loose_dry_density_source; compacted_dry_density; compacted_dry_density_unit; compacted_dry_density_source; temperature; temperature_unit; conversion_factor; conversion_unit; conversion_source; water_source; water_event_id; supplied_water; directly_abstracted_water; incoming_moisture_water; soil_water_retained; water_closing; contained_wastewater; discharge_water; evaporated_water; water_destination; receiving_compartment; water_balance_uncertainty; uncertainty | Reconcile native SKU receipts, calibrated survey/weighing, issued/returned quantities and physical stocktake. Retain actual moisture/density states and sources; join soil/roll/pipe/layer records. | cp_atom_req_cap_cap_water_release | cap | atom_req_cap_cap_water_release | mandatory_refinement | parent_requirement_id; actual_child_row_id; single_object_or_true_mixed_carrier; sku_or_service_result_or_species; physical_state_or_gate; process_id; direction; flow_type; one_exchange_property; one_exchange_unit; receiving_compartment; source_or_destination; event_id; period_id; measurement_point; timestamp; independent_quantity; quantity_evidence; auxiliary_measurements; supported_conversion; provider_or_assembly_scope; overlap_owner; stock_fate_ledger_id; actual_absence_evidence; unresolved_reason; completion_status | Enumerate the actual child roster under this requirement; bind every independently measured event to one child/material+property+unit+state/medium group. Use cp_cap_emission with its original raw fields and method for each applicable compatible group, not as an undifferentiated parent total. Close input/output/stock/fate and paired transfers per same object/unit; retain parent totals only as nonadditive diagnostics. Whole mixed waste carrier and its included contaminant views are nonadditive. Record actual absence or unresolved evidence explicitly; never fill unknown with zero. All original preparation/rework/period/provider/asset and Range-evidence duties remain mandatory. | One explicit child unit per separately indexed record | Each actual child/event and complete phase close | Original full boundary and all applicable periods | cap | per reference flow | Roster/quantity/unit/identity/scope/closure missing = incomplete; check is methodological, not automatic proof of site completion |


### Calculation Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Formula or rule | Inputs | Output | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| atom_final3_quantity_semantics | all actual children and typed residual requirements | For each actual child, compute its own unnormalized numerator q_child by its stated role and one exchange unit before dividing once by A. Material stock supply S is attributed opening plus external net-tare receipts minus burden-carried closing, with the original single gross-with-outward-flows or consumed-only true usable-return convention; nonreturned waste/loss keeps upstream supply. Outward usable return, actual waste and internal handoff quantities are independently recorded, not the input S. An actual Count-equivalent asset child uses q_child=(actual job/node compatible driver / evidenced all-consumer complete-life same-driver denominator) Item(s), with full burden per complete asset Item(s); never one whole asset per job, a material-stock formula, a rental-hour/fee conversion or another life division. A current Duration/h service instead uses actual attributable service h and complete applicable burden per the same h, never multiplies its h by the asset fraction again; complete provider coverage is acceptable, actual missing phases/resources must be added once without hypothetical fuel/assets. A completed-service-event child uses only actual independently accepted physical results whose contract/reference really supports Item(s), never labour, fees, hours or count of reports as a proxy. Any incompatible native service contract stays unresolved under its requirement until an exact single-unit method is reviewed. Species/media release children require actual species-state-medium and independent measured or validated kg; a carrier total or unknown balance is not a release. H2O vapour specifically uses independently evidenced H2O kg to the applicable air compartment; water-volume raw ledgers require an actual compatible H2O-content/state/density bridge and remain nonadditive. For actual owned asset and provider service overlaps select one burden owner and one quantity view. A is the original positive certified unique net developed final-cover surface in m2, with the actual slope/survey convention. Layers, overlaps, rolls, repairs, coupons and prepared-subgrade handoff area never increase A. | actual_child_row_id; q_child; one_exchange_unit; original accepted denominator; original compatible node CP; actual source evidence | q_child per original reference flow |  |
| normalize_area | all inventory rows | Original supplier native quantities are raw auxiliary records. Every actual current exchange uses its own explicitly stated single property/unit under atom_final3_typed_child_routes and atom_final3_quantity_semantics, with an actual same-identity/state conversion only where evidenced. Unknown remaining references are refinement requirements, not cards with selectable units. The denominator is the original positive certified unique net developed A in m2; no layer/overlap/roll/repair or prepared-subgrade area increases it. | attributable_native_quantity; A; cp_cap_gate | Selected single-unit quantity per 1 m2 certified final cover |  |
| native_state | prep_soil_grading_soil; atom_req_prepare_prep_soil actual-child group; cap_soil_soil_barrier; cap_soil_gas_mineral; cap_soil_drain_mineral; cap_soil_protection_soil; cap_soil_vegetative_medium; atom_req_cap_cap_soil actual-child group; atom_req_prepare_prepare_fuel actual-child group; atom_req_cap_cap_fuel actual-child group; erosion_vegetation_seed; erosion_vegetation_mulch; erosion_vegetation_fertilizer; erosion_vegetation_tackifier; erosion_vegetation_soil_amendment; atom_req_cap_erosion_vegetation actual-child group | Convert only compatible actual states: wet_mass = wet_volume * wet_density; dry_mass = wet_mass * (1 - wet_basis_water_fraction); loose_volume * loose_dry_density = compacted_volume * compacted_dry_density for the same retained dry solids after separately recorded additions and losses. Dry-basis moisture requires dry_mass = wet_mass / (1 + dry_basis_water_ratio). Retain actual units, temperature, density/moisture method, batch, sources and uncertainty. These are alternative nonadditive views; do not equate loose and compacted volumes or add water twice. | wet_volume; wet_density; wet_mass; wet_basis_water_fraction; dry_basis_water_ratio; loose_volume; loose_dry_density; compacted_volume; compacted_dry_density | Compatible reconciled native-state quantities |  |
| fuel_fates | atom_req_prepare_prepare_fuel actual-child group; atom_req_cap_cap_fuel actual-child group | Supplied fuel upstream is distinct from measured burned fuel for emissions. Use directly measured burn OR audited complete disjoint native-unit fates: S = burned + usable_outward_return + contained_nonburned_waste + uncontained_loss, after closing stock already removed in S. Retain independent uncertainty and investigate unknown loss; never infer combustion from an incomplete residual. Total spill is audit-only; recovered spill retained in closing is already closing. | S; burned; usable_outward_return; contained_nonburned_waste; uncontained_loss; carried_closing; cp_prepare_energy; cp_cap_energy | Measured burn and disjoint reconciled fates |  |
| species_release | atom_req_prepare_prepare_air actual-child group; atom_req_prepare_prepare_water_soil actual-child group; atom_req_cap_cap_air actual-child group; atom_req_cap_cap_water_soil actual-child group | species_mass = measured_species_mass OR matched_activity * complete_species_factor OR measured_whole_fluid_release * evidenced_species_fraction * evidenced_release_fate. Choose one basis per event/species/medium; do not multiply a complete species factor by composition/fate again. Require actual factor numerator/denominator units, source, validity, composition/fate source and measured incremental attribution. Whole-fluid and total-spill views are nonadditive. | measured_species_mass; matched_activity; complete_species_factor; measured_whole_fluid_release; evidenced_species_fraction; evidenced_release_fate | Actual species mass by receiving medium |  |
| freight_work | atom_req_prepare_prepare_freight actual-child group; atom_req_cap_cap_freight actual-child group | transport_work_kg_km = sum(actual_load_mass_kg * actual_loaded_distance_km); retain actual route, mode, load state, required empty-return activity and coverage. If native upstream t*km is used, 1 t*km = 1000 kg*km. Do not add fuel/vehicle burdens already inside a complete freight service. | actual_load_mass_kg; actual_loaded_distance_km; empty_return_activity; upstream_scope | Compatible actual mass-distance transport work |  |

### Data Quality Requirements

| requirement_id | Applies to | Requirement | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| atom_final3_source_limitations | all processes | The retained historical Rose Hill CQA citation rid-em-rosehill-cqa-2006 is a source lead with site/jurisdiction applicability limits. Its full original PDF is unavailable in this offline revision, so no new material, layer, numeric criterion, test frequency, thickness or performance claim is verified from that unseen original. Preserve this source gap and verify the original before using it as sole evidential support. Actual applicable permit, approved closure design and CQA plan provide current site-specific LLDPE/composite-layer eligibility, construction/testing and certification criteria; historical case values are not defaults. Candidate method revision does not assert actual site evidence is complete. | rid-em-rosehill-cqa-2006 full original pending; actual permit/design/CQA records |
| atom_final3_typed_child_routes | all actual child and legacy-role records | Each listed current child has exactly the stated exchange property/unit, node/direction/type and method, with its own original measured operands. The legacy parent name is only a nonexchange role/raw-prefix selector, not a surviving card or a scalar quantity. Index every original raw stock, return/fate, q_scope, helper and closure record by actual_child_row_id, exact identity/SKU, state/medium, event, period, source and owner. ROW_UNIT(actual_child_row_id) reads that real child card. Apply the original compatible role-specific method to that child operands, never the parent sum; unknown actual roster or unsupported conversion/method/unit remains incomplete and cannot be decided by downstream guessing. Different units are separate groups; same-object auxiliary Count/Mass/Volume or energy views are nonadditive, with actual same-identity/state geometry/count/net-mass/density/moisture/temperature sources for conversion. Existing closed material/state and water-fate QA may sum only compatible measured physical portions once, with carrier/component/water lineage, never publish a diagnostic parent exchange. Material supply uses stock S; actual usable outward returns use measured same-SKU/unit custody exits; real waste uses measured whole actual carrier state/receiver; historic removed objects are existing baseline, not new manufacture. Internal send/receipt uses separately evidenced same-object/state/unit/event/gate quantities; equality is reconciliation, not overwriting records or inferring a child from a parent. All original gates, failed/repeated operations, all periods, carrying burden and stock/WIP obligations continue. | atom_req_prepare_prep_soil | prepare | Mandatory actual-child refinement for prepare/inputs/product. Enumerate the distinct actual products, single-function complete services/assets, physical waste carriers or substance/medium releases covered by this role. Preserve these original applicability and evidence obligations: Record each imported soil grade in actual native Mass/kg or Volume/m3 as supplied and its delivered wet/dry, loose/compacted state; existing in-place waste and intermediate cover are baseline, not newly supplied soil. Internal redistribution is an activity record, not another purchase. Range evidence need: Collect actual Approved grading and subgrade soil supplied native-SKU receipt, matched return, stock and installed-state records, supplier specification and measurement uncertainty. Layer design/overlaps, moisture/density or piece dimensions are actual project evidence, never a default rate. Original quantity method, applied separately to each compatible child/material+property+unit/state ledger: Actual attributable native quantity divided once by A, recorded per reference flow from the linked node protocol, with compatible stock and physical-state reconciliation. Keep independently supported named child cards; the remainder is not silently absent. Each actual child has one explicit exchange property/unit and independently collected quantity with exact identity/support before final foreground completion. Do not guess a SKU, species, medium, ratio or unit; no heterogeneous total, copied parent quantity/Range or duplicate whole-carrier/component flow. cp_atom_req_prepare_prep_soil is required; missing roster/identity/unit/quantity/scope/closure keeps the foreground package incomplete, while candidate methodology may be revised without site data. | Original node protocol cp_prepare_stock; independent actual-child roster and evidence |

## 9. Validation Rules

| rule_id | Applies to | Rule | source_ids |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| acceptance_area | all processes | Require A > 0, unique surveyed net developed surface polygons, declared slope measurement convention, exact certification extent and date, signed acceptance, defects closed and functional_unit.reference_flow_link = certified_final_cover. Overlaps, roll supplies, GCL/drain areas, prepared subgrade, coupons, repairs and repeated tests cannot increase A. Unfinished or interim protected zones cannot be accepted by default. | `epa-mswlf-closure-requirements`; `frtr-landfill-soil-capping`; `rid-em-rosehill-cqa-2006` |
| layer_quality | all processes | Require actual approved LLDPE plus low-permeability GCL or soil composite contact design, layer traceability and sequence, qualified subgrade, actual plan-required soil/layer and seam/repair/penetration/anchorage/drainage/gas test records, erosion/vegetation gate, as-builts and responsible certification. No borrowed historical case thickness/frequency/acreage or universal technology performance. Soil-only ET cover, new bottom liner and bare roll supply fail this scope. | `epa-mswlf-closure-requirements`; `frtr-landfill-soil-capping`; `rid-em-rosehill-cqa-2006` |
| ledger_completeness | all processes | Require native SKU supply/return/waste/stock closure, compatible soil moisture-density states, fuel fate closure, actual species-medium release basis and baseline-versus-incremental attribution. Own provider and shared asset upstream scope across all consumers/periods; one representation and normalization only. Unknown material activity, identity, factor, return, life denominator or subcontractor resource coverage blocks a completed dataset; record performed/skipped checks and uncertainty. | `epa-mswlf-closure-requirements`; `frtr-landfill-soil-capping`; `rid-em-rosehill-cqa-2006` |
| rework_period | all processes | Join node, cell/job, batch/panel/weld, event, campaign and period IDs. Reconcile prepare reconditioning and cap repair/retest loops, retained repairs versus waste/returns, temporary gas/water management, shared setup/cleaning and replacements/termination. A late accepted phase does not erase earlier unsuccessful resources; postclosure care stays separately disclosed. Resolve each concrete exchange identity and support unit before final exchange export; candidate semantic guidance is not publication-ready exchange data. | `epa-mswlf-closure-requirements`; `frtr-landfill-soil-capping`; `rid-em-rosehill-cqa-2006` |

## 10. Published Dataset Profile

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| dataset_role | Foreground data package for completed certified final landfill closure-cover service |
| downstream_use | secondary_dataset; background_dataset; process; lifecyclemodel |
| allowed_use | Declared compatible completed final-cover construction service per m2 at certification |
| excluded_use | New landfill/bottom liner, daily/intermediate/soil-only ET covers, landfill operation, later care, verified long-term avoided gas/leachate or ecological benefit claims |
| required_metadata | Cell/site/job and phase dates, retained baseline, actual closure/CQA plan and jurisdiction, LLDPE and barrier/component layer design, survey/slope convention and A, signed gate, resource ownership, upstream service and asset scope |
| required_quality_disclosure | Performed/skipped checks, unresolved concrete identities/supports, source limitations, measured versus factor data, stock/fuel/state reconciliation, incremental attribution, life denominator, uncertainty and missing coverage |
| update_trigger | Changed closure design, material SKU, baseline/gas-water interface, CQA/certification gate, area convention, provider coverage, emission evidence or asset life/replacement/termination evidence |

## 11. Data Sources

| source_id | type | reference | used_for |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| epa-mswlf-closure-requirements | official_guidance | https://www.epa.gov/landfills/requirements-municipal-solid-waste-landfills-mswlfs | Closure plan, completed closure certification and separate postclosure care; actual applicable jurisdiction only. |
| frtr-landfill-soil-capping | official_guidance | https://www.frtr.gov/matrix-2019/Landfill-and-Soil-Capping/ | Project-specific cap layers, barrier/drain/gas functions and installation concerns; no historical numeric design defaults. |
| rid-em-rosehill-cqa-2006 | official_guidance | https://semspub.epa.gov/work/01/293114.pdf | RIDEM/Louis Berger, Rose Hill Landfill Superfund Site Phase II Remedial Action CQA Plan, 2006, introduction and contents: site-specific LLDPE/GCL/composite-drain cap and certification route, not universal MSWLF criteria. |
